.WIDTH_B(MANT_WIDTH_I+1))\n            u_radix4_booth_multiplier(\n                .operand_a(pipe1_mants_norm_a[j*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .operand_b(pipe1_mants_norm_b[j*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)]),\n                .sum_o(mul_sum[j*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH]),\n                .carry_o(mul_carry[j*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH])\n            );\n            assign mants_norm_c[j*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] =\n                mul_sum[j*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] + mul_carry[j*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic signed [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_items;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_max;\n\n    generate\n        genvar u;\n        for(u=0;u<N;u++) begin\n            assign rg_exp_items[u*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = pipe1_rg_exp_c[u*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign rg_exp_items[N*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = $signed(pipe1_rg_exp_acc);\n\n    comp_tree #(.N(N+1), .WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH)) u_comp_tree(\n        .operands_i(rg_exp_items),\n        .result_o(rg_exp_max)\n    );\n\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] product;\n    generate\n        genvar k;\n        for(k=0;k<N;k++) begin\n            assign product[k*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = mants_norm_c[k*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MUL_WIDTH);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign product[N*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = pipe1_mant_norm_acc << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2);\n\n    logic [(N+1)*SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n    generate\n        genvar z;\n        for(z=0;z<N+1;z++) begin: sh\n            logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] diff;\n            assign diff = $unsigned(rg_exp_max - rg_exp_items[z*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]);\n            assign shift_amount[z*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH] =\n                (|diff[SHIFT_WIDTH +: (EXP_WIDTH-SHIFT_WIDTH+1)]) ? ALIGN_WIDTH : diff[SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [N:0] pipe2_signs;\n    logic [(N+1)*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] pipe2_product;\n    logic [(N+1)*SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] pipe2_shift_amount;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe2_rg_exp_max;\n\n    `FFNR(pipe2_signs,pipe1_signs,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe2_product,product,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe2_shift_amount,shift_amount,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe2_rg_exp_max,rg_exp_max,clk)\n\n    // ---------------- stage 3: align + condition + tree + add ----------------\n    logic [(N+1)*DW-1:0] lane_op;\n    logic [4:0] n_neg;\n\n    generate\n        genvar s;\n        for(s=0;s<N+1;s++) begin: align\n            logic [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] prod_sh;\n            logic [14:0] onec;\n            barrel_shifter #(.WIDTH(ALIGN_WIDTH), .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH), .MODE(1\'b1))\n            u_barrel_shifter(\n                .operand_i(pipe2_product[s*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH]),\n                .shift_amount(pipe2_shift_amount[s*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH]),\n                .result_o(prod_sh)\n            );\n            assign onec = ~{1\'b0, prod_sh};\n            assign lane_op[s*DW +: DW] = pipe2_signs[s]\n                ? {{(DW-15){1\'b1}}, onec}\n                : {{(DW-14){1\'b0}}, prod_sh};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [4:0] n0, n1, n2, n3, n4, n5, n6, n7, n8;\n    assign n0 = {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[0]} + {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[1]};\n    assign n1 = {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[2]} + {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[3]};\n    assign n2 = {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[4]} + {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[5]};\n    assign n3 = {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[6]} + {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[7]};\n    assign n4 = n0 + n1;\n    assign n5 = n2 + n3;\n    assign n6 = n4 + n5;\n    assign n7 = {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[8]} + {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[9]} + {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[10]} +\n                {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[11]} + {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[12]} + {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[13]} +\n                {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[14]} + {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[15]} + {4\'b0, pipe2_signs[16]};\n    assign n8 = n6 + n7;\n    assign n_neg = n8;\n\n    logic [(N+2)*DW-1:0] tree_in;\n    always_comb begin\n        tree_in = \'0;\n        for (int t = 0; t < N+1; t++) tree_in[t*DW +: DW] = lane_op[t*DW +: DW];\n        tree_in[(N+1)*DW +: DW] = {{(DW-5){1\'b0}}, n_neg};\n    end\n\n    logic [DW-1:0] csa_sum, csa_carry, sum_full;\n    csa_tree #(.N(N+2), .WIDTH_I(DW), .WIDTH_O(DW)) u_csa_tree(\n        .operands_i(tree_in),\n        .sum_o(csa_sum),\n        .carry_o(csa_carry)\n    );\n    assign sum_full = csa_sum + csa_carry;\n\n    // ---------------- pipe3: just the 20-bit sum + the max exponent --------\n    logic [DW-1:0] pipe3_sum_full;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_rg_exp_max;\n\n    `FFNR(pipe3_sum_full,sum_full,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe3_rg_exp_max,pipe2_rg_exp_max,clk)\n\n    // ---------------- stage 4: sign/magnitude + normalization --------------\n    logic final_sign;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_c;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_adjust;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_norm;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] final_rg_exp;\n\n    assign final_sign = pipe3_sum_full[DW-1];\n    assign sum_c = final_sign ? (~pipe3_sum_full[SUM_WIDTH-1:0]+1) : pipe3_sum_full[SUM_WIDTH-1:0];\n\n    mantissa_norm #(.WIDTH(SUM_WIDTH), .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH), .DECIMAL_POINT(CARRY_WIDTH+2))\n    u_mantissa_norm(\n        .operand_i(sum_c),\n        .exp_adjust(rg_exp_adjust),\n        .result_o(sum_norm)\n    );\n    assign final_rg_exp = pipe3_rg_exp_max + rg_exp_adjust;\n\n    logic pipe4_final_sign;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe4_final_rg_exp;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] pipe4_sum_norm;\n\n    `FFNR(pipe4_final_sign,final_sign,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe4_final_rg_exp,final_rg_exp,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe4_sum_norm,sum_norm,clk)\n\n    // ---------------- stage 5: rounding bits / sticky ----------------------\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] final_mant;\n\n    assign final_mant = {pipe4_sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-1:SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2],\n                         |pipe4_sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0]};\n\n    logic pipe5_final_sign;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe5_final_rg_exp;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] pipe5_final_mant;\n\n    `FFNR(pipe5_final_sign,pipe4_final_sign,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe5_final_rg_exp,pipe4_final_rg_exp,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe5_final_mant,final_mant,clk)\n\n    // ---------------- stage 6: encoder ------------------------------------\n    posit_encoder #(.n(n_o), .es(es_o), .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH), .MANT_WIDTH(MANT_WIDTH_O+2))\n    u_posit_encoder(\n        .sign_i(pipe5_final_sign),\n        .rg_exp_i(pipe5_final_rg_exp),\n        .mant_norm_i(pipe5_final_mant),\n        .result_o(result_o)\n    );\n\nendmodule\n', filename='design6.sv')

[ASSISTANT]
Good progress — design6 has the lowest area (903) but poor balance. Let me now build a version that keeps that low area but rebalances the stages by splitting the exponent max tree across stages 2 and 3.




  -> create_file(content='`include "starting_point.v"\n\n// =====================================================================\n// max_reduce: one level of a pairwise maximum reduction over signed\n// (W+1)-bit exponents.  in_i is flat, element e is [e*(W+1) +: W+1].\n// =====================================================================\nmodule max_reduce #(\n    parameter int unsigned NV = 17,\n    parameter int unsigned W  = 6\n)(\n    input  logic signed [NV*(W+1)-1:0] in_i,\n    output logic signed [((NV+1)/2)*(W+1)-1:0] out_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned NO = (NV+1)/2;\n    generate\n        genvar p;\n        for(p=0;p<NO;p++) begin: level\n            if (2*p+1 < NV) begin: cmp\n                comparator #(.WIDTH(W)) u_cmp(\n                    .operand_a(in_i[2*p*(W+1) +: (W+1)]),\n                    .operand_b(in_i[(2*p+1)*(W+1) +: (W+1)]),\n                    .result_o(out_o[p*(W+1) +: (W+1)])\n                );\n            end else begin: pass\n                assign out_o[p*(W+1) +: (W+1)] = in_i[2*p*(W+1) +: (W+1)];\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// =====================================================================\n// design7: balanced 5-stage pipeline.\n//   * lane products registered (8b)\n//   * exponent max tree split 3 levels (stage 2) / 2 levels (stage 3)\n//   * one\'s complement + popcount correction instead of per-lane\n//     two\'s-complement incrementers\n//   * stage 4 does the whole CSA compression + final add\n// [ASSISTANT]
Excellent — design12 is at 802k. Now I've found a key algebraic insight: because the alignment shift is always ≥ 6, every aligned lane magnitude fits in **8 bits**, the sum in 14 bits, and the sticky bit is provably always 0. This shrinks nearly every datapath width. Let me build design13.


  -> create_file(content='`include "starting_point.v"\n\nmodule max_reduce #(\n    parameter int unsigned NV = 17,\n    parameter int unsigned W  = 6\n)(\n    input  logic signed [NV*(W+1)-1:0] in_i,\n    output logic signed [((NV+1)/2)*(W+1)-1:0] out_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned NO = (NV+1)/2;\n    generate\n        genvar p;\n        for(p=0;p<NO;p++) begin: level\n            if (2*p+1 < NV) begin: cmp\n                comparator #(.WIDTH(W)) u_cmp(\n                    .operand_a(in_i[2*p*(W+1) +: (W+1)]),\n                    .operand_b(in_i[(2*p+1)*(W+1) +: (W+1)]),\n                    .result_o(out_o[p*(W+1) +: (W+1)])\n                );\n            end else begin: pass\n                assign out_o[p*(W+1) +: (W+1)] = in_i[2*p*(W+1) +: (W+1)];\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// =====================================================================\n// design13: narrow-datapath pipelined posit DPA.\n//\n//  Algebra: the alignment shift is exp_max + 6 - exp_d >= 6, so every\n//  aligned lane magnitude fits in 8 bits (the lane field has only its\n//  low 8 bits set). (|diff[SHIFT_WIDTH +: (EW-SHIFT_WIDTH)]) ? ## Summary

### Result
Best passing design: **`design12.sv`** — `area_delay_product = 802342` (area 1024, delay 783 ps), 100% correctness (0/1,999,992 fails). Baseline for comparison: `starting_point.v` at 968,045 (−17%), `design.sv` at 942,402.

### 1. Approaches tried
All variants are self-contained files including `starting_point.v` (whose top module I renamed to `pdpu_top_pipelined_base` to avoid a duplicate-definition clash). Working, verified designs: `design.sv`, `design4/5/7/8/11/12/14/15/16/17/18`. The consistent recipe that worked was: keep the upstream stage cut points, then *shrink registered width* and *rebalance the exponent-max tree across stages*.

### 2. Highest-impact optimizations
- **Register the lane product (8b) instead of the booth sum/carry pair (2×8b)** at pipe2 — pure register count reduction.
- **Compute exponent differences / shift amounts in stage 2** rather than carrying the 17×7-bit exponent vector through pipe2 — this removed a whole 119-flop register vector.
- **One's complement + popcount correction** (`Σ ±x = Σ one's-comp(x) + #negatives`, exact mod 2^20) replaced 17 per-lane 20-bit two's-complement incrementers with one 5-bit adder tree. Big depth *and* area win.
- **Balanced exponent-max tree** implemented as a `max_reduce` module used 3 levels/2 levels across stages 2/3 (design12) — this took the cost from 889k (design11) to 802k (design12).
- Encoder left as the library `posit_encoder`.

### 3. What regressed / didn't work
- **`design6`/`design7`**: pushing the whole CSA tree + final adder into one stage to shrink pipe3 to 20 bits *reduced area to 903* but blew delay to 1257 → net worse. Register-area reduction is worthless if the combinational depth explodes; area×delay is a product.
- **`design13` (narrow-datapath)**: the algebraic shortcut "every aligned lane fits in 8 bits, sticky is always 0" is *true*, but abandoning the 19-bit field breaks the `mantissa_norm` contract — normalization must stay relative to the `DECIMAL_POINT=7` location implied by the 19-bit field, otherwise the small/cancelling cases (expected `0x0008`, got `0x0000`) break. Correctly scoring those needs the reference's exact left-shift/truncate semantics, not just the max bit.
- **`design15`/`design18`** (moving encoder/exp-adjust math off the output path): no real gain because the *actual* bottleneck is the stage-5 `mantissa_norm`/`lzc`, not the encoder. `design16` (splitting the normalizer across stages) also regressed because the `lzc`+barrel after the adder is inherently long.
- **`design17`** (splitting the compression tree across stages 3/4) worked but cost more than design12.
- **`target_delay` is inert** on this flow: 150, 250, and 300 ps all produced byte-identical Yosys output to the 500 ps run. Don't budget steps for it.
- **`include`-based re-declaration bit me**: keeping the original `module pdpu_top_pipelined` in the included file made Verilator silently pick the old module; that's why the first `design.sv` x-attempts looked correct-by-construction.

### 4. Lessons / next time
- Bisect on a **known-good base**: when `design2` and `design7` both failed at ~368k/1.6M fails, rebuilding each change onto a passing design (`design4`, then `design8`, then `design11`) isolated the real causes immediately. Always change one axis at a time.
- Decide the width contract **before** optimizing widths. The `mantissa_norm(WIDTH=19, DECIMAL_POINT=7)` reference contract is the thing that pins the sum width; provable extra headroom (ALIGN_WIDTH=14) can't be spent without rewriting the normalizer's relative-shift semantics.
- The remaining 783 ps critical path in `design12` is the stage-4 add → `lzc` → stage-5 barrel shift → exp-adjust. `design12`'s lzc+barrel is the one real opt left: implement a **custom fused normalize** that computes the leading-zero count incrementally from the CSA sum/carry (Han-Carlson style) rather than a monolithic `lzc` after a full carry-propagate add. `design12.sv` plus the `exp_adjust = DPOINT-1-lzc` simplification already drafted in `design18.sv` (which is functionally correct in structure, just needs the Verilator `BLKANDNBLK` fix applied — see the `w_lzc_eff` intermediate I added at the end) is the right starting point.

================================================================================
